# spring-core: Phase 1 checklist

Generated by `flint api checklist` from `api/upstream/spring-framework-v7.1.0.json` (spring-framework v7.1.0, module `spring-core`), packages `org.springframework.core`, `org.springframework.core.annotation`, `org.springframework.core.convert.**`, `org.springframework.core.env`, `org.springframework.core.io`, `org.springframework.core.io.support`, `org.springframework.core.type`, `org.springframework.lang`, `org.springframework.util`.

Do not edit this file. Classify elements in `api/framework/mapping.json` (docs/spring/api-mapping.md), then regenerate it. A status reads `ported`, `adapted` with its rules, or `excluded` with its category, and is empty while the element is open. The Id column is the key for the mapping: a type's id, or `#signature` after its type's id.

233 types, 2348 elements: 645 ported, 44 adapted, 1659 excluded, 0 open.
